Florida (FL) FCIC                                       Disclaimer Statement

Transport:
TCPIP
FL Specific Transport

Routing and Configuration Information:
Save Byte Routing (Primary)
Mnemonic (Secondary)

 The FCIC assigned ORI for a device (or for the site) should be placed in the
    <Authentication>/<ORI> field in the ConnectCIC header.

 The FCIC assigned Mnemonic for a device should be placed in the
    <Authentication>/<Mnemonic> field in the ConnectCIC header.

 The assigned Social Security Number should be placed in the
    <Authentication>/<SSN> field in the ConnectCIC header. If this is not provided,
    ConnectCIC will default to <Authentication>/<UserName>.

 A user name or user identifier up to 30 characters in length to represent the user
    submitting the request should be placed in the <Authentication/UserName> field in
    the ConnectCIC Header. This value will be placed by ConnectCIC into the FDLE
    Attention field with each request. This value may be overridden on each request by
    sending the transaction's <Requestor> field.

Security Requirements:
Follow CJIS Security Requirements.

Logon Requirements:
None

Certification Requirements:
Agency sponsor is required.

As of 2007, all vendors who will have access to secure data will have to submit
fingerprints to your sponsor agency and take an on-line certification test before the
vendor will be able to implement an interface to FCIC. The portal you will be provided
access to has a training section and you get an hour for the test. The certification is good
for 2 years. CommSys has completed our testing.

Data-Mined Transactions:
NCIC (QA, QB, QG, QV, QW) and DMV (Person and Vehicle)

3. (In/Out) LicensePlateNumber
4. (In/Out) LocalIdNumber
5. (In/Out) Name, BirthDate
6. (In/Out) Name, FBINumber
7. (In/Out) Name, MiscellaneousNumber
8. (In/Out) Name, SocialSecurityNumber
9. (In/Out) Name, OperatorLicenseNumber
10. (In/Out) Name, LicensePlateNumber
11. (In/Out) Name, VehicleIdentificationNumber
12. (In/Out) Name, OriginatingAgencyCaseNumber
13. (In/Out) NCICNumber
14. (In/Out) OwnerAppliedNumber
15. (In/Out) ProcessControlNumber
16. (In/Out) SerialNumber
17. (In/Out) StateIdNumber
18. (In/Out) VehicleIdentificationNumber

The following fields are optional on any of the above combinations:
[BirthDate, FBINumber, MiscellaneousNumber, SocialSecurityNumber, OperatorLicenseNumber,
SexCode, RaceCode, LicensePlateNumber, LicensePlateStateCode,
VehicleIdentificationNumber, VehicleMake, ImageIndicator, StateIdNumber,
DepartmentCorrectionsNumber, JuvenileJusticeNumber, LocalIdNumber,
RelatedHitSearchIndicator, ExpandedNameSearchCode, SerialNumber, Requestor]
